Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a technologies algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 813. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 181/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to technologies theory, we decompose the problem into 813 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 181. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)
